			slidesPerGroup: 2,
		}
	}
});
$('a[data-toggle="tab"]').on('shown.bs.tab', function(e) {
	var paneTarget = $(e.target).attr('href');
	var $thePane = $('.tab-pane' + paneTarget);
	var paneIndex = $thePane.index();
	if($thePane.find('.swiper-container1').length > 0 && 0 === $thePane.find('.swiper-slide-active').length) {
		swiper1[paneIndex].update();
	}
});
</script>
<script>
$('#latest').click(function() {
	$("#latest-link").show();
		$("#best-sales-link").hide();
});
$('#best').click(function() {
	$("#best-sales-link").show();
	$("#latest-link").hide();
});

</script>
